# germany-payroll: figure and rule ledger

Guide: [germany-payroll](../skills/international/germany/germany-payroll.md). Accepted Guide SHA-256: `16b1a73defe4c7e4971d7982ccea5713766875abb49b8ac7edb902545d2ff075`.
Repackaged from the completed [independent country review](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/blob/bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf/ledgers/germany-2026-evidence/independent-review/report.md) at `bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf`. The Guide bytes, all current ledger claims and all original-topic destinations are preserved. This is a packaging change; it does not claim a new legal review or accountant attestation.
Original published ledger SHA-256: `ecc38d2f98ea5b23f41bf8bc615f9ce2d45d4c295d7a4087171ef4ea68e21c9e`. Full source captures, earlier versions, scripts and review chains remain in the original archive; none is added by this replacement. Source excerpts below retain their actual read dates. Short excerpts identify the previously reviewed operative unit; they do not replace its complete conditions.

| ID | Section/nativeLF | Exact current wording | Source IDs/disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| 001 | Scope LF[22, 22] | For employers, payroll clerks and employees checking a German payslip for pay periods in 2026. This is the full-cycle payroll skill: besides the rules shared with `de-payroll`, it covers the payslip, the minimum wage duties, tax-free night, Sunday and holiday surcharges, sick pay and the maternity top-up paid through payroll, and the accident insurance annual report. Covered: wage tax (Lohnsteuer) by tax class and ELStAM through the finance ministry's program flow (PAP), solidarity surcharge and church tax, the new exemption for employees past the standard retirement age; the four social insurance branches (RV, KV, PV, AV) with shares and ceilings; Minijobs and the transition band; the wage tax return, contribution and DEÜV deadlines, year-end and late surcharges; and a short section for 2025 pay still being corrected. | [R62-70.NIGHT](#q-f51c7e9d5dd75c), [R62-70.NIGHTSV](#q-d0b8674c04d9d9), [R62-70.UVREPORT](#q-7b87ba9d6e0d3a). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 002 | Scope LF[24, 24] | The year: the 2026 PAP covers pay periods "die nach dem 31. Dezember 2025, aber vor dem 1. Januar 2027 enden" and one-time payments received in 2026 ([PAP 2026, Anlage 1](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Steuerarten/Lohnsteuer/Programmablaufplan/2025-11-12-PAP-2026-anlage-1.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2)). Social insurance values apply from 1 January 2026. | [A.PAPYEAR](#q-2445493897cb56), [A.PAPVALUES](#q-dc3798db2a8aee). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 003 | Scope LF[26, 26] | Not covered, see "When to refuse or refer": the self-employed (use `de-freelance-intake`), cross-border and treaty cases, managing directors whose insurance status is open, employer pensions (Versorgungsbezüge), benefits in kind and flat-rate taxes under § 37b and § 40 EStG, the miners' pension scheme, and the exact Midijob formulas. Also out of scope: annual leave entitlements, parental leave and Elterngeld, Krankengeld and Mutterschaftsgeld paid by the health fund, and the employee's own income tax return. | Preserved scope/routing/precise limitation; no new positive method.. Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 004 | Scope LF[28, 28] | The employee owes the wage tax; the employer must withhold it at every wage payment ([§ 38 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__38.html)) and is liable for it ([§ 42d(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__42d.html)). The employer pays the total social insurance contribution and ordinarily recovers the employee's share by deduction from pay, subject to the § 28g exceptions stated below ([§ 28e SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28e.html), [§ 28g SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28g.html)). | [A.TAXDUTY](#q-41781b1f259e8c), [A.LIABILITY](#q-15bea15f221151), [A.TOTALSV](#q-0c54517e408c18), [A.RECOVER](#q-4c5ca09864af86), [R62-70.RECOVER](#q-55da1fb0ce1b6d). Bounded R62–70 own-source condition / metadata successor |

| 018 | The method, step by step LF[50, 50] | 1. **Employer number.** Before the first registration, apply electronically to the Federal Employment Agency (its Betriebsnummern-Service) for a Betriebsnummer for each place of employment; separate numbers for different activities or municipalities ([§ 18i SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__18i.html)). | [A.NUMBER](#q-d404497e0a5db9). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 019 | The method, step by step LF[51, 51] | 2. **Status.** Decide Minijob, short-term job, transition band, normal employment, or pay above the compulsory health insurance threshold. Add up several jobs as [§ 8(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html) and [§ 20(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__20.html) say. | [A.MINI](#q-4ed92975f1738a), [A.MIDI](#q-2bc41466d67b50). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 020 | The method, step by step LF[52, 52] | 3. **Immediate report in listed sectors.** Construction, hospitality, passenger transport, haulage and logistics (including platform delivery), fairground work, building cleaning, trade-fair construction, meat industry (not the butchers' trade, "mit Ausnahme des Fleischerhandwerks"), prostitution, security, hairdressing and cosmetics: report the start day to the pension insurers' data office "spätestens bei dessen Aufnahme" ([§ 28a(4) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28a.html)). It does not replace the registration in step 6. | [A.IMMEDIATE](#q-8208fb42e07dcd). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 021 | The method, step by step LF[53, 53] | 4. **Wage tax data.** Take the tax ID, date of birth, and whether this is the first or a further job; retrieve the ELStAM, enter them in the payroll account, and call up changes "monatlich" ([§ 39e(5) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39e.html)). Missing data by the employee's fault: class VI. A technical fault or a reason the employee is not responsible for: the expected class for at most three calendar months ([§ 39c(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39c.html)). If identification number/date of birth remains missing after the three calendar months, apply class VI retrospectively under § 39c(1) sentence 3. Once actual ELStAM arrive for the temporary-data cases, review/correct prior months as necessary and settle under- or over-withholding at the next payroll (§ 39c(1) sentences 4–5). | [A.ELSTAM](#q-64c06cfdc06e84), [A.MISSING](#q-c3025649b31149), [R93.missing](#q-98a4af6644a8e7). R88–96 bounded own-source selector/computation/operational successor; R62–70 gates retained |
| 022 | The method, step by step LF[54, 54] | 5. **Payroll account.** One Lohnkonto per employee and year at the place of business, recording pay (tax-free pay included) and tax withheld at every payment; keep it to the end of the sixth calendar year after the last entry ([§ 41(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__41.html)). For social insurance, keep pay records per employee and year in Germany and in German until the end of the calendar year after the last audit; an employer without a seat in Germany appoints an authorised person there ([§ 28f(1), (1b) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28f.html)). | [A.TAXACCOUNT](#q-5bdb8d241c81aa), [A.SVACCOUNT](#q-a0b0122e92c778). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 023 | The method, step by step LF[55, 55] | 6. **Registration** with the collecting office: the employee's health fund, or the Minijob-Zentrale for a Minijob ([§ 28i SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28i.html)). Deadlines for this and every later report are in "Filing and payment". | [A.FUND](#q-63cfb1fc225994). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 024 | The method, step by step LF[57, 57] | **Every pay period** | Preserved scope/routing/precise limitation; no new positive method.. Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 025 | The method, step by step LF[59, 59] | 7. **Wage tax.** Take out tax-free pay first (such as the Aktivrente). Run the 2026 PAP on the rest, then the surcharge and, for church members, church tax on the base BK. One-time payments use the annual method. | [A.AKT](#q-1e1eec6c8a96a9), [A.WITHHOLD](#q-7d73827b98e3d7), [A.CHILDBASE](#q-6d7e131bd5f088). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 026 | The method, step by step LF[60, 60] | 8. **Contributions.** Cap pay at each branch's monthly ceiling, apply the shares, deduct the employee's. Under § 28g SGB IV the ordinary claim is recovered by deduction from pay. A missed deduction can ordinarily be recovered at the next three payments, and later if the employer was not at fault. The deduction-only and three-payment restrictions do not apply where the employee intentionally or grossly negligently breaches § 28o(1), bears the entire total social contribution, or receives only benefits in kind. ([§ 28g SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28g.html)). | [A.RECOVER](#q-4c5ca09864af86), [A.CEILING](#q-45419c8313c8cc), [A.RVCEILING](#q-c4d76d979b8a68), [R62-70.RECOVER](#q-55da1fb0ce1b6d). Bounded R62–70 own-source condition / metadata successor |

| 120 | Tax-free pay past the standard retirement age (Aktivrente), new in 2026 LF[197, 197] | - **How much.** Up to the monthly amount in the table; the yearly amount falls by one twelfth for each month the conditions are not met. Unused room does not move to another month; one-time payments are exempt only within the month's room; severance as a rule is not covered. Pay for periods before the qualifying month is never covered, whenever it is paid. Pay already tax-free under another rule does not use it (§ 3 no. 21 sentence 2). | [A.AKT](#q-1e1eec6c8a96a9), [A.AKTHOW](#q-e824ed80e536ba), [A.AKTPART](#q-2e728952a9e779). | 145 | Rates LF[233, 233] | - **What counts.** Children who have turned 25 do not count toward the reductions; the reduction for a child ends at the end of the month it turns 25. Parents, at any age of the child, do not pay the surcharge. The surcharge does not apply to people born before 1 January 1940, to people doing military or civilian service, or to recipients of basic income support ([§ 55(3) SGB XI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_11/__55.html)). | [A.CAREPROOF](#q-27c8aa03fca53c). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 146 | Rates LF[234, 234] | - **Proof.** Parenthood and the number of children under 25 must be proven to the employer unless already known. Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |

| 155 | Ceilings and thresholds LF[248, 248] | - **The threshold decides who may leave statutory health insurance.** When pay rises above the JAEG, compulsory insurance ends only at the end of that calendar year, and only if pay also exceeds the threshold for the next year ([§ 6(4) SGB V](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_5/__6.html)). | [A.JAEG](#q-f01d6c00cf21fc). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 156 | Ceilings and thresholds LF[249, 249] | - **2027 change already enacted.** From 2027 the health ceiling will be the special threshold plus EUR 3,600 ([§ 223(4) SGB V](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_5/__223.html): "entspricht der um 3 600 Euro erhöhten Jahresarbeitsentgeltgrenze nach § 6 Absatz 7 im Jahr 2027"). The 2027 amounts are set by regulation in late 2026 (check). | [A.KV2027](#q-a17ed82c3b8a24). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 157 | Contributions in practice LF[253, 253] | - **Base.** For each branch: pay for the period, capped at that branch's monthly ceiling. Apply the employee and employer shares from the tables. Round each amount to the cent. | [A.RVCEILING](#q-c4d76d979b8a68), [A.CEILING](#q-45419c8313c8cc), [A.HSHARE](#q-f150c72bdd132a), [A.PENSIONSHARE](#q-1927bbd7a0bc7e), [A.AVSHARE](#q-bc026ddd04eb1d), [A.CARESHARE](#q-e8478b726065f1). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 158 | Contributions in practice LF[254, 254] | - **Several jobs.** If pay from several insured jobs together passes a ceiling, each pay is first cut to the ceiling and then reduced in proportion so that together they reach the ceiling ([§ 22(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__22.html)). The second employer does not stop paying. | [A.SEVERAL](#q-2f6e98944dbbb3). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 159 | Contributions in practice LF[255, 255] | - **One-time payments.** They belong to the period in which they are paid and carry contributions only as far as pay so far in the year has not used up the pro-rata yearly ceiling. A payment from 1 January to 31 March that would pass that ceiling goes to the last period of the previous year, if the same employer employed the person then. For those compulsorily insured in health insurance the health ceiling is the test ([§ 23a SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__23a.html)). | [A.SVMARCH](#q-1a11a1ef2b89d4). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 160 | Contributions in practice LF[256, 256] | - **Employees past the standard retirement age.** Drawing a full old-age pension after reaching that age makes the job pension-free, but the employer still pays its half ([§ 172(1) SGB VI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_6/__172.html)); unemployment insurance works the same way after that age ([§ 346(3) SGB III](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_3/__346.html)). Where no employer pension contribution is due, the Aktivrente does not apply. | [A.RVOLD](#q-c9b9348401e949), [A.AVOLD](#q-2830b3e6edf7c8), [A.AKT](#q-1e1eec6c8a96a9). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |

| 166 | Employer-only levies LF[267, 267] | U1, U2 and the insolvency levy are charged on the pay on which pension contributions are worked out ([§ 7(2) AAG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aufag/__7.html), [§ 358(2) SGB III](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_3/__358.html)). For U1, exclude one-time pay under § 23a SGB IV and pay for employment lasting no longer than four weeks where the nature of the job prevents sickness-pay entitlement under § 3(3) EntgFG. Do not transfer that U1 exclusion to U2 or the insolvency levy. During short-time allowance, AAG levies use actual earned pay up to the pension ceiling (§ 7(2) AAG). For U1 reimbursement, apply the documented eligible-pay and employer-contribution scope of the actual scheme; do not assume the same base for both. On stipulated eligible continued pay EUR 1,000, an applicable 80% wage-reimbursement tariff gives EUR 800 and an applicable 60% tariff gives EUR 600 ([§ 9(2) no. 1 AAG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aufag/__9.html)). | [A.AAGBASE](#q-f2e2dca8d57ad3), [A.INSOLVBASE](#q-65f40e3bdaefb2), [R62-70.AAGBASE](#q-f9368b65cfb252), [R90.tariff](#q-3a461144990f8f). | 186 | Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band LF[293, 293] | - **Regular pay.** The limit is on regular monthly pay. An unforeseeable overrun in not more than two calendar months within a twelve-month period, each time by up to the limit itself, does not end the Minijob ([§ 8(1b) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html)). | [A.MINI](#q-4ed92975f1738a). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 187 | Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band LF[294, 294] | - **Several jobs.** Several Minijobs are added together. One Minijob beside a main insured job stays separate; every further Minijob is added to the main job ([§ 8(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html)). If aggregation shows that the minor-employment conditions fail, insurance begins on the day the competent insurance decision is notified under § 8(2) SGB IV, unless the employer intentionally or grossly negligently failed to investigate the facts needed to assess insurance status. This is the specific aggregation rule, not a universal bar on retroactive insurance. | [A.MINI](#q-4ed92975f1738a), [R62-70.MINI](#q-057788b94b3bd1). Bounded R62–70 own-source condition / metadata successor |
| 188 | Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band LF[295, 295] | - **Pension duty is the rule.** A Minijobber is compulsorily insured in pension insurance and pays the own share unless they apply, in writing or electronically, to be released ([§ 6(1b) SGB VI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_6/__6.html)). If released, the employer still pays its flat share. | [A.MINIRELEASE](#q-340e206338dc1f), [A.RVOLD](#q-c9b9348401e949). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 189 | Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band LF[298, 298] | - **Flat tax is an option.** The employer may instead tax the pay by the ELStAM. The 2% flat tax is collected by Deutsche Rentenversicherung Knappschaft-Bahn-See, the Minijob-Zentrale ([§ 40a(6) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__40a.html)); the 20% and 25% flat taxes are declared with the wage tax return. | [A.FLATTAX](#q-955224cb0c7d68). Exact original current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 190 | Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band LF[299, 299] | - **Short-term jobs.** A job limited in advance to three months or 70 working days in a calendar year, or 15 weeks or 90 working days for employment in an agricultural business is contribution-free unless done as a profession with pay above the Minijob limit ([§ 8(1) no. 2 SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html)). The social insurance test and the 25% tax test are different tests. | [A.MINI](#q-4ed92975f1738a), [A.FLATTAX](#q-955224cb0c7d68), [R62-70.MINI](#q-057788b94b3bd1). | 213 | Tax-free night, Sunday and holiday surcharges LF[339, 339] | The two caps differ: a surcharge can be tax-free and still carry contributions. Public holidays are those at the place of work. Sunday and holiday work also includes 00:00–04:00 on the following day if the night work began before midnight (§ 3b(3) no. 2 EStG). With EUR 30 hourly basic pay and an otherwise qualifying 25% night premium, EUR 7.50 is tax-free within the EUR 50 tax ceiling; EUR 6.25 is contribution-free and EUR 1.25 remains contributory. Actual work, additional payment and applicable statutory percentage gates still apply. | [A.NIGHT](#q-2e2f9679cad8de), [A.NIGHTSV](#q-ceb811e8ed7690), [R62-70.NIGHT](#q-f51c7e9d5dd75c), [R62-70.NIGHTSV](#q-d0b8674c04d9d9). | 214 | Sick pay and the maternity top-up LF[343, 344] | - **Continued pay in sickness.** For incapacity due to sickness without employee fault, up to six weeks per incapacity, at the pay for regular working time without overtime pay, once the job has lasted four weeks without a break. The same illness again gives a new six weeks only after six months without incapacity from it, or twelve months after the first incapacity from it began ([§ 3 EntgFG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/entgfg/__3.html), [§ 4 EntgFG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/entgfg/__4.html)). Small employers in U1 get part of it back (80% statutory default, subject to the actual fund statute/selected tariff under § 9(2) no. 1 AAG, with a 40% floor). Under § 4(1a) EntgFG, additional overtime pay is excluded, as are expense reimbursements contingent on actual expenses that do not arise during incapacity. For performance-related remuneration, use the average earnings achievable during the employee's applicable regular working time.<br>- **Maternity top-up.** For the protection periods before and after the birth and the day of birth, the employer pays the difference between EUR 13 and the average net pay per calendar day of the last three settled calendar months before the protection period ([§ 20(1) MuSchG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/muschg_2018/__20.html)).  U2 refunds it in full ([§ 1(2) AAG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aufag/__1.html)). For the ordinary multiple-employer case, add the relevant employers' average daily net wages, subtract EUR 13 once, then allocate that top-up in proportion to each employer's average daily net wage. At independently established daily net EUR 60 and EUR 40, combined EUR 100 less EUR 13 gives EUR 87; employer shares are EUR 52.20 and EUR 34.80, not two separate EUR 13 deductions. The ordinary worksheet assumes ongoing employment and established § 20 net inputs. New employment begun during protection is covered from its start under § 20(1); the specific authorised-dismissal/insolvency cases shift payment to the statutory paying agency under § 20(3). For treaty non-residents whose residence state has the taxing right, § 20(4) uses only social-insurance deductions unless that state does not tax the top-up, in which case all statutory deductions count. This is not a full § 21 proof of each real net-pay input. | [A.SICK](#q-0c41bca3bf8aee), [A.SICKPAY](#q-b81844c09a3358), [A.AAG](#q-7a55bb6735e80e), [R62-70.SICK](#q-b414a727a754c6), [R62-70.SICKPAY](#q-bc237127b1317f), [R90.tariff](#q-3a461144990f8f), [R94.maternity](#q-ac12f34d8dc709). R88–96 bounded own-source selector/computation/operational successor; R62–70 gates retained |

| 320 | 2025 pay still being corrected LF[498, 498] | The 2025 annual report was due by Monday 16 February 2026: nominal 15 February was a Sunday, so § 26(3) SGB X moves the ordinary deadline to the next working day. This does not override an authority notice expressly fixing the day under that provision or an authority-set appointment under § 26(5). The [actual social-procedure rule](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_10/__26.html) supplies this adjustment; AO § 108 is not its authority. The 2025 wage tax certificates were due by the last day of February 2026 (2 March 2026, as 28 February 2026 was a Saturday). A late or corrected certificate is still sent electronically. Employees with wages from two employers at once in 2025 must file a 2025 return ([§ 46(2) no. 2 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)). | [A.ANNUAL](#q-bd88eb0abecde8), [A.CERTDUE](#q-f97e50a017a967), [A.AO108](#q-682cfd69e6549c), [A.FILE2](#q-fa9faf0a95fd64), [R69.SGBX26](#q-1c28e9dac6a8ff), [R69.ANNUAL](#q-40f221cda8bd07). | 347 | R88–96 exact ordinary selector / labelled computation / duty LF[297, 297] | **Minimum pension base for a small insured Minijob.** For an isolated full-month pension-insured commercial Minijob, with no other insured employment, exemption or part-month/aggregation interaction, § 163(8) SGB VI uses at least EUR 175. Apply the full 18.6% to max(actual monthly pay, EUR 175), then subtract the employer's rounded 15% share of actual pay to obtain the employee remainder (§ 168(1) no. 1b SGB VI; § 2(1) BVV). At actual pay EUR 100, total pension is EUR 32.55, employer EUR 15.00 and employee EUR 17.55, rather than EUR 3.60. Other-job, exemption and part-month cases require their actual assessment facts; do not apply a universal separate EUR 175 floor to every job. | [R89.minimum](#q-6da8686e065df7), [R89.employershare](#q-16e96af03174cb), [R89.calculation](#q-24074032f97520). New finite own-source condition, not a new general payroll audit |
| 348 | R88–96 exact ordinary selector / labelled computation / duty LF[473, 473] | \| Employee copy of social-report content \| At least annually by 30 April, in text form with the current or at latest next payslip, separately or attached. Solely accident-insurance-data-change reports are excepted. On termination issue the certificate immediately after the last report, rather than waiting for annual 30 April \| [§ 28a(5) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28a.html) \| | [R96.copy](#q-b69e4e68b48e11). | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF22 | For employers, payroll clerks and employees checking a German payslip for pay periods in 2026. This is the full-cycle payroll skill: besides the rules shared with `de-payroll`, it covers the payslip, the minimum wage duties, tax-free night, Sunday and holiday surcharges, sick pay and the maternity top-up paid through payroll, and the accident insurance annual report. Covered: wage tax (Lohnsteuer) by tax class and ELStAM through the finance ministry's program flow (PAP), solidarity surcharge and church tax, the new exemption for employees past the standard retirement age; the four social insurance branches (RV, KV, PV, AV) with shares and ceilings; Minijobs and the transition band; the wage tax return, contribution and DEÜV deadlines, year-end and late surcharges; and a short section for 2025 pay still being corrected. | Exact wording retained currentLF22, Scope Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF24 | The year: the 2026 PAP covers pay periods "die nach dem 31. Dezember 2025, aber vor dem 1. Januar 2027 enden" and one-time payments received in 2026 ([PAP 2026, Anlage 1](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Steuerarten/Lohnsteuer/Programmablaufplan/2025-11-12-PAP-2026-anlage-1.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2)). Social insurance values apply from 1 January 2026. | Exact wording retained currentLF24, Scope Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF26 | Not covered, see "When to refuse or refer": the self-employed (use `de-freelance-intake`), cross-border and treaty cases, managing directors whose insurance status is open, employer pensions (Versorgungsbezüge), benefits in kind and flat-rate taxes under § 37b and § 40 EStG, the miners' pension scheme, and the exact Midijob formulas. Also out of scope: annual leave entitlements, parental leave and Elterngeld, Krankengeld and Mutterschaftsgeld paid by the health fund, and the employee's own income tax return. | Exact wording retained currentLF26, Scope Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF28 | The employee owes the wage tax; the employer must withhold it at every wage payment ([§ 38 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__38.html)) and is liable for it ([§ 42d(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__42d.html)). The employer pays the total social insurance contribution and recovers the employee's share only by deduction from pay ([§ 28e SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28e.html), [§ 28g SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28g.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF28, Scope Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF44 | Defaults when an answer is missing, for an estimate only: unknown class, estimate in class I, but real payroll uses class VI while the employee is at fault for missing data ([§ 39c EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39c.html)); unknown church, none; unknown children, childless surcharge if 23 or older; unknown Saxony flag, outside Saxony; unknown insurance markers, insured in all branches; unknown additional rate, the average rate (never for a real payslip). | Exact wording retained currentLF44, Ask the client first Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF50 | 1. **Employer number.** Before the first registration, apply electronically to the Federal Employment Agency (its Betriebsnummern-Service) for a Betriebsnummer for each place of employment; separate numbers for different activities or municipalities ([§ 18i SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__18i.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF50, The method, step by step Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

Add up several jobs as [§ 8(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html) and [§ 20(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__20.html) say. | Exact wording retained currentLF51, The method, step by step Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF52 | 3. **Immediate report in listed sectors.** Construction, hospitality, passenger transport, haulage and logistics (including platform delivery), fairground work, building cleaning, trade-fair construction, meat industry (not the butchers' trade, "mit Ausnahme des Fleischerhandwerks"), prostitution, security, hairdressing and cosmetics: report the start day to the pension insurers' data office "spätestens bei dessen Aufnahme" ([§ 28a(4) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28a.html)). It does not replace the registration in step 6. | Exact wording retained currentLF52, The method, step by step Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF53 | 4. **Wage tax data.** Take the tax ID, date of birth, and whether this is the first or a further job; retrieve the ELStAM, enter them in the payroll account, and call up changes "monatlich" ([§ 39e(5) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39e.html)). Missing data by the employee's fault: class VI. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF54 | 5. **Payroll account.** One Lohnkonto per employee and year at the place of business, recording pay (tax-free pay included) and tax withheld at every payment; keep it to the end of the sixth calendar year after the last entry ([§ 41(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__41.html)). For social insurance, keep pay records per employee and year in Germany and in German until the end of the calendar year after the last audit; an employer without a seat in Germany appoints an authorised person there ([§ 28f(1), (1b) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__28f.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF54, The method, step by step Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

Deadlines for this and every later report are in "Filing and payment". | Exact wording retained currentLF55, The method, step by step Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF169 | - **Pension:** pay up to the pension ceiling times the employee pension share, unless the employee is not in the statutory scheme (PAP marker KRV = 1). | Exact wording retained currentLF169, Vorsorgepauschale (insurance deduction inside the tax) Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF170 | - **Health:** pay up to the health ceiling times half the reduced health rate plus half the fund's own additional rate. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF171 | - **Care:** pay up to the health ceiling times the employee care share from the social insurance table. | Exact wording retained currentLF171, Vorsorgepauschale (insurance deduction inside the tax) Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF172 | - **Private insurance:** instead of health and care, the monthly private premiums from the ELStAM less the tax-free employer subsidy (classes I to V only). | Exact wording retained currentLF172, Vorsorgepauschale (insurance deduction inside the tax) Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF173 | - **Unemployment:** pay up to the pension ceiling times the employee share, only if insured (ALV = 0) and not in class VI, and only so far as it and the health and care parts together do not pass the cap in the table. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF192 | If the employee has not handed in the certificates of earlier employers this year, scale the current pay up for those months, mark the payroll account with the capital letter S, and the employee must then file a return ([§ 46(2) no. 5a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)). | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF196 | - **Who.** Employees in a job under § 19(1) no. 1 EStG, from the month after they reach the standard retirement age of § 35 or § 235 SGB VI, where the employer owes pension contributions for that work ([§ 3 no. 21 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__3.html)). A pension need not be drawn. Minijobs, self-employment and civil service pay are not covered ([finance ministry FAQ](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/FAQ/FAQ-zur-Aktivrente.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF196, Tax-free pay past the standard retirement age (Aktivrente), new in 2026 Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF197 | - **How much.** Up to the monthly amount in the table; the yearly amount falls by one twelfth for each month the conditions are not met. Unused room does not move to another month; one-time payments are exempt only within the month's room; severance as a rule is not covered. Pay for periods before the qualifying month is never covered, whenever it is paid. Pay already tax-free under another rule does not use it (§ 3 no. 21 sentence 2). | Exact wording retained currentLF197, Tax-free pay past the standard retirement age (Aktivrente), new in 2026 Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF198 | - **Payroll.** The employer must apply it and leave the exempt pay out of the wage tax. Class VI only with the employee's confirmation that no other job uses it, kept in the payroll account. When a job ends or the employer changes mid-month, the amount is apportioned by days on a 30-day month; a new job started mid-month gets the full amount if the employee confirms in writing that no Aktivrente was used that month. Contributions are unchanged. See Case 5. | Exact wording retained currentLF198, Tax-free pay past the standard retirement age (Aktivrente), new in 2026 Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF233 | - **What counts.** Children who have turned 25 do not count toward the reductions; the reduction for a child ends at the end of the month it turns 25. Parents, at any age of the child, do not pay the surcharge. The surcharge does not apply to people born before 1 January 1940, to people doing military or civilian service, or to recipients of basic income support ([§ 55(3) SGB XI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_11/__55.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF233, Rates Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF234 | - **Proof.** Parenthood and the number of children under 25 must be proven to the employer unless already known. Proof through the automated procedure, or other proof given within six months of the birth, counts from the month of birth; later proof counts from the month after it is given ([§ 55(3a) SGB XI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_11/__55.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF234, Rates Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

One value for the whole country. | Exact wording retained currentLF247, Ceilings and thresholds Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF248 | - **The threshold decides who may leave statutory health insurance.** When pay rises above the JAEG, compulsory insurance ends only at the end of that calendar year, and only if pay also exceeds the threshold for the next year ([§ 6(4) SGB V](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_5/__6.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF248, Ceilings and thresholds Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF249 | - **2027 change already enacted.** From 2027 the health ceiling will be the special threshold plus EUR 3,600 ([§ 223(4) SGB V](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_5/__223.html): "entspricht der um 3 600 Euro erhöhten Jahresarbeitsentgeltgrenze nach § 6 Absatz 7 im Jahr 2027"). The 2027 amounts are set by regulation in late 2026 (check). | Exact wording retained currentLF249, Ceilings and thresholds Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF253 | - **Base.** For each branch: pay for the period, capped at that branch's monthly ceiling. Apply the employee and employer shares from the tables. Round each amount to the cent. | Exact wording retained currentLF253, Contributions in practice Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF254 | - **Several jobs.** If pay from several insured jobs together passes a ceiling, each pay is first cut to the ceiling and then reduced in proportion so that together they reach the ceiling ([§ 22(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__22.html)). The second employer does not stop paying. | Exact wording retained currentLF254, Contributions in practice Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF255 | - **One-time payments.** They belong to the period in which they are paid and carry contributions only as far as pay so far in the year has not used up the pro-rata yearly ceiling. A payment from 1 January to 31 March that would pass that ceiling goes to the last period of the previous year, if the same employer employed the person then. For those compulsorily insured in health insurance the health ceiling is the test ([§ 23a SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__23a.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF255, Contributions in practice Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF256 | - **Employees past the standard retirement age.** Drawing a full old-age pension after reaching that age makes the job pension-free, but the employer still pays its half ([§ 172(1) SGB VI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_6/__172.html)); unemployment insurance works the same way after that age ([§ 346(3) SGB III](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_3/__346.html)). Where no employer pension contribution is due, the Aktivrente does not apply. | Exact wording retained currentLF256, Contributions in practice Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF293 | - **Regular pay.** The limit is on regular monthly pay. An unforeseeable overrun in not more than two calendar months within a twelve-month period, each time by up to the limit itself, does not end the Minijob ([§ 8(1b) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF293, Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF294 | - **Several jobs.** Several Minijobs are added together. One Minijob beside a main insured job stays separate; every further Minijob is added to the main job ([§ 8(2) SGB IV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__8.html)). | Exact wording retained currentLF294, Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF295 | - **Pension duty is the rule.** A Minijobber is compulsorily insured in pension insurance and pays the own share unless they apply, in writing or electronically, to be released ([§ 6(1b) SGB VI](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_6/__6.html)). If released, the employer still pays its flat share. | Exact wording retained currentLF295, Minijobs, short-term jobs and the transition band Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF296 | - **Flat tax is an option.** The employer may instead tax the pay by the ELStAM. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF413 | The table is built with ALV, KRV and PKV = 0, KVZ = 2,90 and the childless surcharge (class II without it). Software implementing the [2026 PAP](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Steuerarten/Lohnsteuer/Programmablaufplan/2025-11-12-PAP-2026-anlage-1.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2) must reproduce it, and should also pass these checks: the wage tax is the same with and without child counters, while the surcharge and BK never rise with them; the class V tax is never below 14% of the taxable amount and is above class I in every row; pay above both ceilings changes no contribution; a surcharge base just above the limit gives 11.9% of the excess, below 5.5% of the base, and nothing at or below the limit. | Exact wording retained currentLF415, Case 4: second job in class VI, and the ministry's test table Actual R88–96 gates/one-source-derived calendar date qualify this same retained topic where relevant; R62–70 scope retained. |

Wages from two employers at once oblige the employee to file a return ([§ 46(2) no. 2 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)).
